5:## Summary
8:## Users
13:## Scope
26:## Features
28:### Universe membership (`UNIV`)
34:- **UNIV-01** The system returns the S&P 500 member list as of any trading date in the covered history.
37:- **UNIV-02** Members that were later removed or delisted appear on the dates they were members.
39:- **UNIV-03** Each stock carries a permanent identifier that never changes and is never reused.
42:- **UNIV-04** Membership history is reproduced: it agrees with publicly announced index changes.
50:### Price & corporate-action ingest (`INGST`)
56:- **INGST-01** A full download fetches the complete history for every stock that was ever a member.
58:- **INGST-02** An update download fetches only data newer than the last successful run, plus any vendor revisions.
60:- **INGST-03** Every download produces an immutable, dated snapshot that later runs never overwrite.
62:- **INGST-04** A vendor revision to an already-stored record is kept as a new version with both values on record.
64:- **INGST-05** A run that fails partway leaves the last good snapshot untouched and can be resumed.
66:- **INGST-06** Every run records its start and end time, scope, record counts, and any errors.
74:### Download validation (`VALID`)
80:- **VALID-01** No snapshot becomes the current dataset until validation passes.
82:- **VALID-02** Price records are checked for internal consistency.
84:- **VALID-03** Every member stock has a bar on every exchange trading day of its membership, or a recorded reason (halt, suspension) for the gap.
86:- **VALID-04** Large daily moves must be explained.
88:- **VALID-05** Corporate actions are applied correctly.
90:- **VALID-06** Membership integrity is checked on every snapshot.
92:- **VALID-07** Each promoted snapshot gets a data-quality report produced with the `data-analysis` skill.
94:- **VALID-08** Duplicate records are rejected.
101:### Point-in-time data access (`PANEL`)
107:- **PANEL-01** An as-of-T request never returns information published after T's close.
110:- **PANEL-02** A returns panel for a date range contains only stocks that were members on each date.
112:- **PANEL-03** A delisted stock's final return, through its last price or delisting payout, is included.
115:- **PANEL-04** Missing values stay missing.
117:- **PANEL-05** Every request can be pinned to a snapshot so results can be reproduced.
119:- **PANEL-06** Daily risk-free rates are available for the same dates as prices.
126:### Data explorer (`EXPL`)
132:- **EXPL-01** The researcher can view the member list for any covered date.
134:- **EXPL-02** The researcher can open any stock ever in the universe, delisted ones included.
136:- **EXPL-03** A stock page shows adjusted price, daily return and volume over time, with splits, dividends, membership periods and delisting marked.
138:- **EXPL-04** The researcher can browse validation results for any snapshot.
140:- **EXPL-05** The researcher can see data coverage across the universe over time.
142:- **EXPL-06** Charts and layouts are built with the `dataviz` and `frontend-design` skills.
149:### Live run monitor (`RUNS`)
155:- **RUNS-01** A running download or validation shows live progress.
157:- **RUNS-02** Failures appear while the run is going.
159:- **RUNS-03** Past runs are listed with their records.
161:- **RUNS-04** Opening the monitor partway through a run shows the run's state so far.
168:### Walk-forward engine (`WFWD`)
174:- **WFWD-01** One schedule defines the test periods for every method.
176:- **WFWD-02** Test periods are consecutive blocks of 21 trading days, and the first starts after at least 756 trading days of history.
178:- **WFWD-03** A gap separates each training window from its test period: at least 5 trading days, and never shorter than the longest label horizon the method uses.
180:- **WFWD-04** A method sees only data available by the end of its training window when it is fitted.
182:- **WFWD-05** The most recent 504 trading days are a held-back lockbox, scored once per hypothesis only after its walk-forward result is recorded.
185:- **WFWD-06** The universe in every test period is the membership as of its start.
192:### Hypothesis ledger (`LEDG`)
198:- **LEDG-01** A hypothesis is registered with its claim, null, metric, test, significance level, correction family, snapshot and test periods before it runs.
200:- **LEDG-02** A registered hypothesis cannot be edited once its run starts.
202:- **LEDG-03** Every test is run with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill on the registered metric and test periods.
204:- **LEDG-04** Every outcome is recorded, failures included.
206:- **LEDG-05** No comparison, ranking or claim is reported without a registered ledger entry.
208:- **LEDG-06** The ledger can be listed by stage, feature and outcome.
215:### Simulated portfolio backtest (`BTEST`)
221:- **BTEST-01** Portfolios rebalance at the start of each test period and drift with returns within it.
223:- **BTEST-02** Trading costs of 10 basis points per side are charged on traded value.
225:- **BTEST-03** Each test period reports realised variance, after-cost return, turnover, and Sharpe ratio net of the risk-free rate.
227:- **BTEST-04** A held stock that is delisted is closed at its delisting return.
234:### Factor removal (`FACT`)
240:- **FACT-01** Factors are estimated only on each training window, never the full sample.
242:- **FACT-02** The number of factors is chosen per window: those whose eigenvalues exceed what pure-noise returns of the same shape would produce.
244:- **FACT-03** Residual returns show much less cross-correlation than raw returns.
246:- **FACT-04** Stocks are included only for the dates they have data; nothing is zero-filled.
248:- **FACT-05** Residual returns are available as of any date for later stages.
255:### Correlation denoising (`DENOI`)
261:- **DENOI-01** Noise eigenvalues are replaced and signal eigenvalues are kept.
263:- **DENOI-02** The denoised matrix is a valid correlation matrix.
265:- **DENOI-03** Denoising is fitted only on the training window.
271:### Covariance estimator ranking (`COV`)
277:- **COV-01** Each estimator is scored by the realised out-of-sample variance of the minimum-variance portfolio built from it.
279:- **COV-02** Estimators are characterised with the `data-analysis` skill before ranking.
281:- **COV-03** Each estimator's improvement over the sample estimate is a registered hypothesis tested with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill.
283:- **COV-04** The ranking is published with every estimator's score, effect size and ledger outcome.
289:### Classical portfolios (`PORT`)
295:- **PORT-01** Each portfolio is long-only and fully invested, with no stock above 5% weight.
297:- **PORT-02** Minimum variance, risk parity and hierarchical risk parity are each built from the top-ranked COV estimator.
299:- **PORT-03** Each risk-based portfolio's lower variance versus equal weight is a registered hypothesis tested with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill.
301:- **PORT-04** The ranking reports realised variance, after-cost return, Sharpe ratio and turnover for all four portfolios.
303:- **PORT-05** The minimum-variance portfolio's after-cost results are stored as the benchmark for S4.
305:- **PORT-06** Risk parity gives each stock equal risk contribution under the estimated covariance.
311:### Candidate features (`FEAT`)
317:- **FEAT-01** A feature value on date T uses only data known by T's close.
319:- **FEAT-02** Features are computed for every member on every date where their lookback has enough data, and are missing elsewhere.
321:- **FEAT-03** At least 10 candidate features are defined before S3 testing starts, each with a written rationale.
323:- **FEAT-04** Each feature's distribution and stability are characterised with the `data-analysis` skill before testing.
329:### Feature significance (`SIG`)
335:- **SIG-01** Each feature is tested against the next 5-day residual return from FACT.
337:- **SIG-02** Each feature's information gain is a registered hypothesis tested with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill on the walk-forward test periods.
339:- **SIG-03** The surviving features are recorded as the only engineered inputs S4 may use.
341:- **SIG-04** Every feature's outcome is recorded, failures included.
347:### Return & volatility forecasters (`FCST`)
353:- **FCST-01** Forecast targets are forward log return and forward realised volatility over 1, 5 and 21 trading days, counted in trading days, not rows.
355:- **FCST-02** Training windows respect the walk-forward gap for the longest target horizon.
357:- **FCST-03** Inputs known in advance, observed inputs and fixed attributes are kept separate, and fixed attributes are point-in-time.
359:- **FCST-04** Normalisation statistics come only from each training window.
361:- **FCST-05** The volatility forecaster must beat trailing 63-day realised volatility and an exponentially weighted volatility baseline.
363:- **FCST-06** The return forecaster must beat a zero-return forecast.
365:- **FCST-07** Every training run is reproducible from its recorded snapshot, settings and seed.
372:### Forecast-driven portfolios (`FPORT`)
378:- **FPORT-01** A volatility-forecast portfolio is judged on realised variance against the S2 minimum-variance portfolio.
380:- **FPORT-02** A return-forecast portfolio is judged on after-cost Sharpe ratio against the S2 minimum-variance portfolio.
382:- **FPORT-03** Forecast-driven portfolios meet the same constraints as classical ones.
388:### Trading agents (`AGENT`)
394:- **AGENT-01** Agents train only in simulations built from training-window data.
396:- **AGENT-02** Each agent line registers a budget of at most 20 configurations before training starts.
398:- **AGENT-03** An agent is judged on after-cost Sharpe ratio against the S2 minimum-variance portfolio.
400:- **AGENT-04** An agent line whose budget is used up with no pass stops, and is recorded as failed.
402:- **AGENT-05** Agent actions are valid portfolios.
408:### Final verdict (`VERD`)
414:- **VERD-01** The verdict lists every S4 method with its walk-forward outcome, lockbox outcome and effect size versus the benchmark.
416:- **VERD-02** A method is declared to beat the classical portfolios only if it passed walk-forward and was not significantly worse on the lockbox.
418:- **VERD-03** The verdict states one conclusion: which methods beat the benchmark, or that none does.
424:## Data requirements
425:- **DATA-01** Daily S&P 500 membership history with effective dates for every addition and removal, covering at least 15 years.
427:- **DATA-02** Daily open, high, low, close and volume for every stock ever a member, from 252 trading days before its first membership through its last trading day, delisted stocks included.
429:- **DATA-03** Corporate actions for every such stock: splits, cash dividends, spin-offs, ticker changes and delistings, each with its date, and for delistings the reason and final price or payout where one exists.
431:- **DATA-04** Every record carries the date it became known, as well as the date it refers to.
433:- **DATA-05** The US equity exchange trading calendar for the covered history.
435:- **DATA-06** A daily short-term risk-free interest rate for the covered history.
437:- **DATA-07** A sector classification for every stock, with the dates each classification applied.
440:## Quality requirements
441:- **QR-01** Data costs beyond the existing price subscription are at most $50 a month.
443:- **QR-02** Rented GPU spending is at most $100 a month.
445:- **QR-03** All analysis except neural-network training runs on one laptop: a daily update download plus validation finishes in at most 15 minutes, and a full history rebuild in at most 6 hours.
447:- **QR-04** The browser app is reachable only from the local machine.
449:- **QR-05** Downloaded vendor data never leaves the laptop, apart from training copies sent to rented GPU machines, which are deleted when the rental ends.
451:- **QR-06** Every data shape exchanged between the analysis side and the browser app is declared once, and both sides are checked against it.
453:- **QR-07** Every reported result can be reproduced from its recorded snapshot, settings and seed.
455:- **QR-08** The explorer shows a single stock's full history within 2 seconds and a membership date within 1 second.
458:## Assumptions
467:## Open questions

# data — Pipelines

**Package:** data · **Status:** Draft · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Contract:** [contract.md](contract.md)

## PL-09: Download
**Entry:** `packages/data/src/qrp_data/pipelines/pl09_download.py::run_download` · **Trigger:** `qrp data download --full|--update [--resume RUN]` · **Part of:** PL-01
**Covers:** INGST-01–INGST-06, DATA-01–DATA-04, DATA-06, DATA-07, PANEL-06, UNIV-03, RUNS-01, RUNS-02, QR-01, QR-03

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `open_run` | runs.record | mode, resume → RunRecord | insert the run row in `runs.sqlite` (WAL), heartbeat thread every 2 s | INGST-06, RUNS-01 |
| 2 | `make_sources` | vendors.factory | settings → Source | one provider per vendor from `qrp.toml`; `CredentialsError` on a missing key before any fetch | QR-01 |
| 3 | `SharadarSource` | vendors.sharadar | api key → Source | `get_table` per table and stock, 5 retries with backoff (waits on PB-01) | DATA-01–DATA-03 |
| 4 | `FredSource` | vendors.fred | url → Source | DTB3 CSV, discount rate → bond-equivalent → daily simple rate (AD-04) | DATA-06, PANEL-06 |
| 5 | `EdgarSicSource` | vendors.edgar | user agent → Source | SIC from each annual-report header, ≤ 8 requests/s (waits on PB-02) | DATA-07 |
| 6 | `SyntheticSource` | vendors_synthetic | seed → Source | 20 seeded stocks over 3 years: a split, a dividend, a reused ticker, a cash delisting, a bankruptcy, one revision, a reclassification | DATA-01–DATA-07 |
| 7 | `fetch_universe` | ingest.scope | Source, RunRecord → StockId | SP500 and TICKERS in full, appended as parts; every permaticker ever a member | INGST-01, UNIV-03 |
| 8 | `download_stocks` | ingest.fetch | Source, StockId, RunRecord → RunRecord | per stock in turn: SEP and ACTIONS (since the watermark on `--update`), `store.append_parts` stamps known dates, one event per stock; failures recorded, `--resume` skips finished stocks | INGST-01, INGST-02, INGST-04, INGST-05, DATA-02–DATA-04, RUNS-02 |
| 9 | `download_series` | ingest.series | Source, RunRecord → RunRecord | DTB3 in full; SIC history per CIK; carried rates `filled=True` | DATA-06, DATA-07 |
| 10 | `stage_snapshot` | store.manifest | RunRecord → Snapshot | manifest = previous snapshot's parts + this run's, vendor watermarks, status `staged`; never touches `CURRENT` | INGST-03, INGST-05 |
| 11 | `close_run` | runs.record | RunRecord, Snapshot → RunRecord | end time, counts, errors, status, stage timings | INGST-06, QR-03 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def run_download(mode: Literal["full", "update"], *, resume: str | None = None) -> RunRecord:
    run = open_run(mode, resume=resume)
    sources = make_sources(settings())
    stocks = fetch_universe(sources, run)
    run = download_stocks(sources, stocks, run)
    run = download_series(sources, run)
    snapshot = stage_snapshot(run)
    return close_run(run, snapshot)
```

## PL-10: Validate and promote
**Entry:** `packages/data/src/qrp_data/pipelines/pl10_validate.py::validate_snapshot` · **Trigger:** `qrp data validate <snapshot>`; `qrp data download` runs it on the snapshot it staged · **Part of:** PL-01
**Covers:** VALID-01–VALID-08, UNIV-04, DATA-05, RUNS-01

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `open_run` | runs.record | snapshot → RunRecord | as PL-09, kind `validation` | RUNS-01 |
| 2 | `resolve_snapshot` | store.manifest | SnapshotId → Snapshot | read the manifest; staged, or an older one for revalidation | VALID-01 |
| 3 | `calendar` | calendar | Snapshot → Calendar | `exchange_calendars` XNYS sessions clipped to coverage | DATA-05 |
| 4 | `returns_panel` | panel | Snapshot, dates → ReturnsPanel | PL-11 as built, on the staged snapshot | VALID-04 |
| 5 | `check_prices` | validate.prices | Snapshot → Issue | high ≥ low, open and close inside [low, high], prices > 0, volume ≥ 0 | VALID-02 |
| 6 | `check_gaps` | validate.gaps | Snapshot, Calendar → Issue | a bar per member per session or a recorded halt; SEP dates vs XNYS | VALID-03 |
| 7 | `check_moves` | validate.moves | ReturnsPanel → Issue | \|return\| > 25% with no action or override → flagged | VALID-04 |
| 8 | `check_actions` | validate.actions | Snapshot, ReturnsPanel → Issue | every action day: AD-08 total return vs `closeadj` within 0.01 pp | VALID-05 |
| 9 | `check_membership` | validate.membership | Snapshot, Calendar → Issue | `membership_as_of` per session: 495–510 members; the announced-changes set when present | VALID-06, UNIV-04 |
| 10 | `check_duplicates` | validate.duplicates | Snapshot → Issue | duplicate (stock, ref_date, known_date) rows rejected | VALID-08 |
| 11 | `decide` | validate.quarantine | Issue, RunRecord → ValidationResult | write `issues.parquet`, quarantine failing records, blocking if any check blocks | VALID-01 |
| 12 | `promote` | store.current | ValidationResult → Snapshot | build the sorted read view, atomic rename of `CURRENT`; skipped when blocking or revalidating | VALID-01 |
| 13 | `gather_quality_inputs` | validate.report | Snapshot, ReturnsPanel, Issue → QualityInputs | coverage, issues, returns and overrides of the snapshot | VALID-07 |
| 14 | `write_quality_report` | validate.report | QualityInputs → QualityReport | coverage, issue counts, return tails and overrides, with the `data-analysis` skill | VALID-07 |
| 15 | `close_run` | runs.record | RunRecord, ValidationResult → RunRecord | as PL-09 | RUNS-01 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def validate_snapshot(snapshot: SnapshotId, *, promote: bool = True) -> ValidationResult:
    run = open_run("validation", scope=snapshot)
    snap = resolve_snapshot(snapshot)
    cal = calendar(snap.id)
    returns = returns_panel(*snap.coverage, snapshot=snap.id)
    issues = concat_issues(check_prices(snap), check_gaps(snap, cal), check_moves(returns),
                           check_actions(snap, returns), check_membership(snap, cal), check_duplicates(snap))
    result = decide(issues, run)
    promote_snapshot(result, enabled=promote)
    write_quality_report(gather_quality_inputs(snap, returns, issues))
    close_run(run, result)
    return result
```

## PL-11: Point-in-time returns
**Entry:** `packages/data/src/qrp_data/pipelines/pl11_returns_panel.py::returns_panel` · **Trigger:** a call to `data.returns_panel` from `engine`, `lab` and `models` · **Part of:** PL-03
**Covers:** PANEL-01–PANEL-05, UNIV-02, INGST-04, DATA-05

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `resolve_snapshot` | store.manifest | SnapshotId → Snapshot | as PL-10; `CURRENT` when None, `SnapshotError` when unknown | PANEL-05 |
| 2 | `calendar` | calendar | Snapshot → Calendar | as PL-10 | DATA-05 |
| 3 | `total_returns` | panel.returns | Snapshot, Calendar, dates, as_of → ReturnsPanel | DuckDB over the snapshot's parts: newest version with known_date ≤ as_of, AD-08 total return, member mask from `universe`, delisting return on the last day, NaN elsewhere | PANEL-01–PANEL-04, UNIV-02, INGST-04 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def returns_panel(start: date, end: date, *, as_of: date | None = None,
                  snapshot: SnapshotId | None = None) -> ReturnsPanel:
    snap = resolve_snapshot(snapshot)
    cal = calendar(snap.id)
    return total_returns(snap, cal, start, end, as_of=as_of or end)
```

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def membership_as_of(d: date, *, snapshot: SnapshotId | None = None) -> Membership:
    snap = resolve_snapshot(snapshot)
    cal = calendar(snap.id)
    return members_on(snap, cal, d)
```

## PL-13: Stock history
**Entry:** `packages/data/src/qrp_data/pipelines/pl13_stock_history.py::stock_history` · **Trigger:** a call to `data.stock_history` from `server` · **Part of:** PL-02
**Covers:** UNIV-02, UNIV-03, PANEL-03, PANEL-04

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `resolve_snapshot` | store.manifest | SnapshotId → Snapshot | as PL-11 | PANEL-05 |
| 2 | `calendar` | calendar | Snapshot → Calendar | as PL-10 | DATA-05 |
| 3 | `stock_ref` | universe.identity | StockId, Snapshot → StockRef | TICKERS rows for the permaticker: name, ticker spans, delisting date; `KeyError` when unknown | UNIV-03 |
| 4 | `stock_bars` | panel.history | StockRef, Snapshot, Calendar → StockHistory | one row per session from first to last bar, NaN rows for gaps; actions and membership intervals | UNIV-02, PANEL-03, PANEL-04 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def stock_history(stock: StockId, *, snapshot: SnapshotId | None = None) -> StockHistory:
    snap = resolve_snapshot(snapshot)
    cal = calendar(snap.id)
    ref = stock_ref(stock, snap)
    return stock_bars(ref, snap, cal)
```

## Open questions
- `concat_issues` and `promote_snapshot(enabled=…)` in the PL-10 sketch: should the six checks run as one `run_checks` step returning `Issue`? (assumed: six steps, each with its own upgrade)
- `store.parts`, `universe.search`, `panel.bars`, `panel.rates` and `panel.coverage` hold contract functions that are called from other packages' steps, not from a data pipeline (assumed: modules of their component, listed here so the build plans them)
- Should the quality report be written for rejected snapshots too? (assumed: yes, it explains the rejection)

`qrp data download` runs it on the snapshot it staged · **Part of:** PL-01\n**Covers:** VALID-01–VALID-08, UNIV-04, DATA-05, RUNS-01\n\n| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `open_run` | runs.record | snapshot → RunRecord | as PL-09, kind `validation` | RUNS-01 |\n| 2 | `resolve_snapshot` | store.manifest | SnapshotId → Snapshot | read the manifest; the announced-changes set when present | VALID-06, UNIV-04 |\n| 10 | `check_duplicates` | validate.duplicates | Snapshot → Issue | duplicate (stock, ref_date, known_date) rows rejected | VALID-08 |\n| 11 | `decide` | validate.quarantine | Issue, RunRecord → ValidationResult | write `issues.parquet`, quarantine failing records, blocking if any check blocks | VALID-01 |\n| 12 | `promote` | store.current | ValidationResult → Snapshot | build the sorted read view, atomic rename of `CURRENT`; skipped when blocking or revalidating | VALID-01 |\n| 13 | `gather_quality_inputs` | validate.report | Snapshot, ReturnsPanel, Issue → QualityInputs | coverage, issues, returns and overrides of the snapshot | VALID-07 |\n| 14 | `write_quality_report` | validate.report | QualityInputs → QualityReport | coverage, issue counts, return tails and overrides, with the `data-analysis` skill | VALID-07 |\n| 15 | `close_run` | runs.record | RunRecord, ValidationResult → RunRecord | as PL-09 | RUNS-01 |\n\n### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```python\ndef validate_snapshot(snapshot: SnapshotId, *, promote: bool = True) -> ValidationResult:\n    run = open_run(\"validation\", scope=snapshot)\n    snap = resolve_snapshot(snapshot)\n    cal = calendar(snap.id)\n    returns = returns_panel(*snap.coverage, snapshot=snap.id)\n    issues = concat_issues(check_prices(snap), check_gaps(snap, cal), check_moves(returns),\n                           check_actions(snap, returns), check_membership(snap, cal), check_duplicates(snap))\n    result = decide(issues, run)\n    promote_snapshot(result, enabled=promote)\n    write_quality_report(gather_quality_inputs(snap, returns, issues))\n    close_run(run, result)\n    return result\n```\n\n## PL-11: Point-in-time returns\n**Entry:** `packages/data/src/qrp_data/pipelines/pl11_returns_panel.py::returns_panel` · **Trigger:** a call to `data.returns_panel` from `engine`, `lab` and `models` · **Part of:** PL-03\n**Covers:** PANEL-01–PANEL-05, UNIV-02, INGST-04, DATA-05\n\n| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `resolve_snapshot` | store.manifest | SnapshotId → Snapshot | as PL-10; ## PL-14: Walk-forward schedule
**Entry:** `packages/engine/src/qrp_engine/pipelines/pl14_schedule.py::schedule` · **Trigger:** a call to `engine.schedule` from `lab` and `models` · **Part of:** PL-03
**Covers:** WFWD-01, WFWD-02, WFWD-05

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `calendar` | data | SnapshotId → Calendar | `data.calendar`; the contract's replay double until the system task | DATA-05 |
| 2 | `tile_periods` | walkforward.schedule | Calendar, SnapshotId → Schedule | last 504 sessions are the lockbox; 21-session blocks from session 757 up to it; `ValueError` when too short | WFWD-01, WFWD-02, WFWD-05 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def schedule(snapshot: SnapshotId) -> Schedule:
    cal = calendar(snapshot)
    return tile_periods(cal, snapshot)
```

## PL-15: Backtest
**Entry:** `packages/engine/src/qrp_engine/pipelines/pl15_backtest.py::backtest` · **Trigger:** a call to `engine.backtest` from `lab` and `models` · **Part of:** PL-03
**Covers:** BTEST-01–BTEST-04, PORT-01, WFWD-06

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `returns_panel` | data | Schedule, SnapshotId → ReturnsPanel | `data.returns_panel` over the schedule's periods; replay double until the system task | PANEL-02 |
| 2 | `check_all` | constraints.check | Weights, Schedule, SnapshotId → Weights | `check_weights` per period against `data.membership_as_of(period.start)`; `WeightsError` names period and stock | PORT-01, WFWD-06 |
| 3 | `simulate` | backtest.simulate | Weights, ReturnsPanel, Schedule → BacktestResult | rebalance at each period start, daily drift, 10 bp per side on traded value, a delisted holding closes to cash at its delisting return; Sharpe net of `data.risk_free`; a period with no weights is a failed row | BTEST-01–BTEST-04 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def backtest(weights: Sequence[Weights], *, schedule: Schedule, snapshot: SnapshotId,
             cost_bps: float = 10.0, cap: float | None = 0.05) -> BacktestResult:
    returns = returns_panel(schedule, snapshot)
    checked = check_all(weights, schedule, snapshot, cap=cap)
    return simulate(checked, returns, schedule, cost_bps=cost_bps)
```

## PL-16: Run a registered test
**Entry:** `packages/engine/src/qrp_engine/pipelines/pl16_run_test.py::run_test` · **Trigger:** a call to `engine.run_test` from `lab` and `models` · **Part of:** PL-03
**Covers:** LEDG-02, LEDG-03, LEDG-05, QR-07

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `match_registration` | ledger.freeze | RunHandle, TestInput → Hypothesis | load the frozen registration; `LedgerRefused` when null, metric, family or α differ | LEDG-02 |
| 2 | `family_inputs` | ledger.families | Hypothesis → TestInput | every entry naming the family, abandoned and incomplete ones included, with their recorded inputs | LEDG-03 |
| 3 | `test_hypothesis` | ledger.nhst | TestInput, Hypothesis → TestOutcome | with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill: paired sign-flip permutation on per-period differences, Holm across the family | LEDG-03 |
| 4 | `write_result` | ledger.results | RunHandle, TestOutcome → TestOutcome | `results/<entry>/test.json` with snapshot, config hash, seed and git SHA | LEDG-05, QR-07 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def run_test(run: RunHandle, test_input: TestInput) -> TestOutcome:
    hypothesis = match_registration(run, test_input)
    family = family_inputs(hypothesis)
    outcome = test_hypothesis(test_input, family, hypothesis)
    return write_result(run, outcome)
```

# lab — Pipelines

**Package:** lab · **Status:** Draft · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Contract:** [contract.md](contract.md)

## PL-18: Covariance estimator ranking
**Entry:** `packages/lab/src/qrp_lab/pipelines/pl18_rank_covariance.py::run_rank_covariance` · **Trigger:** `qrp lab rank-covariance [--snapshot ID] [--seed N]` · **Part of:** PL-03
**Covers:** FACT-01–FACT-04, DENOI-01–DENOI-03, COV-01–COV-04, LEDG-01, LEDG-04, LEDG-05
**References:** RF-01 (characterise_factors)

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `current_snapshot` | data | snapshot id → Snapshot | `data.current_snapshot`, or the id given | PANEL-05 |
| 2 | `schedule` | engine | Snapshot → Schedule | PL-14 as built | WFWD-01 |
| 3 | `returns_panel` | data | Schedule, Snapshot → ReturnsPanel | PL-11 as built | PANEL-01 |
| 4 | `start_cov_entries` | rankings.entries | Schedule, Snapshot, seed → RunHandle | register and start one `COV` entry per estimator vs sample (3) | COV-03, LEDG-01, LEDG-05 |
| 5 | `characterise_factors` | factors.characterise | ReturnsPanel, Schedule, RunHandle → Characterisation | `fit_factors` on every 12th window; mean \|corr\| raw vs residual, k per window, with the `data-analysis` skill; as RF-01, per-window fit | FACT-01–FACT-04 |
| 6 | `characterise_denoising` | denoise.characterise | ReturnsPanel, Schedule, RunHandle → Characterisation | `denoise` on the same windows: eigenvalues before and after, edge, T/N | DENOI-01–DENOI-03 |
| 7 | `make_estimators` | covariance.registry | estimator names, seed → CovEstimator | lookup in `lab.ESTIMATORS` (contract) | COV-01 |
| 8 | `SampleCov` | covariance.sample | → CovEstimator | pairwise-complete sample covariance, nearest-PSD projection | COV-01 |
| 9 | `ShrinkageCov` | covariance.shrinkage | → CovEstimator | Ledoit–Wolf toward constant correlation | COV-01 |
| 10 | `FactorCov` | covariance.factor | seed → CovEstimator | `fit_factors` loadings plus diagonal residual variance | COV-01 |
| 11 | `DenoisedCov` | covariance.denoised | seed → CovEstimator | `denoise` the correlation, rescale by window volatilities | COV-01, DENOI-03 |
| 12 | `characterise_estimators` | covariance.characterise | CovEstimator, ReturnsPanel, Schedule, RunHandle → Characterisation | condition number, T/N and eigen-spectra per estimator on the sampled windows, with the `data-analysis` skill | COV-02 |
| 13 | `estimator_weights` | rankings.walk | CovEstimator, ReturnsPanel, Schedule → Weights | per period: `training_window(horizon=1)`, `guard`, fit, `min_variance`; `NotPositiveDefinite` or `WeightsError` → no weights, the period fails | COV-01, WFWD-03, WFWD-04 |
| 14 | `backtest` | engine | Weights, Schedule, Snapshot → BacktestResult | PL-15 as built, per estimator | BTEST-01–BTEST-04 |
| 15 | `test_estimators` | rankings.tests | BacktestResult, RunHandle → TestOutcome | per-period variance paired with sample's into `engine.run_test`; `record_outcome` | COV-03, LEDG-04 |
| 16 | `publish_ranking` | rankings.report | BacktestResult, TestOutcome → Ranking | mean out-of-sample variance order with effect size and outcome; `results/<entry>/ranking.md` | COV-04 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def run_rank_covariance(snapshot: SnapshotId | None = None, *, seed: int = 0) -> Ranking:
    snap = current_snapshot(snapshot)
    sched = schedule(snap.id)
    returns = returns_panel(sched, snap.id)
    runs = start_cov_entries(sched, snap.id, seed=seed)
    characterise_factors(returns, sched, runs)
    characterise_denoising(returns, sched, runs)
    estimators = make_estimators(ESTIMATOR_NAMES, seed=seed)
    characterise_estimators(estimators, returns, sched, runs)
    weights = estimator_weights(estimators, returns, sched)
    results = backtest(weights, sched, snap.id)
    outcomes = test_estimators(results, runs)
    return publish_ranking(results, outcomes)
```

## PL-19: Classical portfolios and the S4 benchmark
**Entry:** `packages/lab/src/qrp_lab/pipelines/pl19_classical.py::run_classical` · **Trigger:** `qrp lab classical` after PL-18's ranking is recorded · **Part of:** PL-04
**Covers:** PORT-01–PORT-06, LEDG-01, LEDG-04

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `current_snapshot` | data | snapshot id → Snapshot | as PL-18 | PANEL-05 |
| 2 | `schedule` | engine | Snapshot → Schedule | PL-14 as built | WFWD-01 |
| 3 | `returns_panel` | data | Schedule, Snapshot → ReturnsPanel | PL-11 as built | PANEL-01 |
| 4 | `top_estimator` | rankings.report | Ranking → CovEstimator | the recorded PL-18 ranking's first estimator; `LedgerRefused` before it exists | PORT-02 |
| 5 | `start_port_entries` | rankings.entries | Schedule, Snapshot, seed → RunHandle | register and start the three `PORT` entries (each risk-based vs equal weight) | PORT-03, LEDG-01 |
| 6 | `portfolio_weights` | portfolios | CovEstimator, ReturnsPanel, Schedule → Weights | per period: window, `guard`, fit; `min_variance` (cvxpy QP), `risk_parity` (Spinu, Newton; fails when the cap binds), `hrp`, `equal_weight` | PORT-01, PORT-02, PORT-06 |
| 7 | `backtest` | engine | Weights, Schedule, Snapshot → BacktestResult | PL-15 as built, per portfolio | BTEST-01–BTEST-04 |
| 8 | `test_portfolios` | rankings.tests | BacktestResult, RunHandle → TestOutcome | paired per-period variance vs equal weight into `engine.run_test`; `record_outcome` | PORT-03, LEDG-04 |
| 9 | `store_benchmark` | rankings.benchmark | BacktestResult, RunHandle → BacktestResult | minimum variance's result to `results/benchmark/<entry>.parquet` | PORT-05 |
| 10 | `publish_portfolios` | rankings.report | BacktestResult, TestOutcome → Ranking | variance, after-cost return, Sharpe and turnover for all four; `results/<entry>/portfolios.md` | PORT-04 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def run_classical(snapshot: SnapshotId | None = None, *, seed: int = 0) -> Ranking:
    snap = current_snapshot(snapshot)
    sched = schedule(snap.id)
    returns = returns_panel(sched, snap.id)
    estimator = top_estimator(cov_ranking())
    runs = start_port_entries(sched, snap.id, seed=seed)
    weights = portfolio_weights(estimator, returns, sched)
    results = backtest(weights, sched, snap.id)
    outcomes = test_portfolios(results, runs)
    store_benchmark(results, runs)
    return publish_portfolios(results, outcomes)
```

## PL-20: Feature significance
**Entry:** `packages/lab/src/qrp_lab/pipelines/pl20_features.py::run_features` · **Trigger:** `qrp lab features` after PL-19 · **Part of:** PL-05
**Covers:** FACT-05, FEAT-01–FEAT-04, SIG-01, SIG-02, SIG-04, LEDG-01, LEDG-04
**References:** RF-01 (residual_returns), RF-03 (forward_targets)

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `current_snapshot` | data | snapshot id → Snapshot | as PL-18 | PANEL-05 |
| 2 | `schedule` | engine | Snapshot → Schedule | PL-14 as built | WFWD-01 |
| 3 | `returns_panel` | data | Schedule, Snapshot → ReturnsPanel | PL-11 as built | PANEL-01 |
| 4 | `bars_panel` | data | Schedule, Snapshot → BarsPanel | `data.bars_panel`; the contract's replay double until the system task | PANEL-01 |
| 5 | `calendar` | data | Snapshot → Calendar | `data.calendar` | DATA-05 |
| 6 | `residual_returns` | factors.residuals | ReturnsPanel, Schedule, seed → ResidualReturns | each date's residuals from the loadings of the latest window ending before it; NaN where the return is; as RF-01, per-window fit | FACT-05 |
| 7 | `forward_targets` | targets | ReturnsPanel, ResidualReturns, Calendar → Targets | session-counted horizons 1, 5, 21; NaN if a session lacks a return; `label_end` per row; as RF-03, sessions not rows | SIG-01, FCST-01 |
| 8 | `compute_features` | features.compute | BarsPanel, FeatureDef → FeaturePanel | the ≥ 10 `lab.FEATURES`, members only, NaN without full lookback; pairs above 0.9 correlation flagged | FEAT-01–FEAT-03 |
| 9 | `characterise_features` | features.characterise | FeaturePanel, Schedule → Characterisation | distribution, tails, missingness and rank stability per feature, with the `data-analysis` skill | FEAT-04 |
| 10 | `start_sig_entries` | significance.entries | FeaturePanel, Schedule, Snapshot, seed → RunHandle | register and start one `SIG` entry per feature, near-duplicates included | SIG-02, LEDG-01 |
| 11 | `rank_ic` | significance.ic | FeaturePanel, Targets, Schedule → TestInput | per period: Spearman IC vs `fwd_resid_5d`, `guard` on `label_end` | SIG-01, WFWD-03 |
| 12 | `test_features` | significance.tests | TestInput, RunHandle → TestOutcome | `engine.run_test` (IC > 0), `record_outcome` for every feature | SIG-02, SIG-04, LEDG-04 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def run_features(snapshot: SnapshotId | None = None, *, seed: int = 0) -> tuple[TestOutcome, ...]:
    snap = current_snapshot(snapshot)
    sched = schedule(snap.id)
    returns = returns_panel(sched, snap.id)
    bars = bars_panel(sched, snap.id)
    cal = calendar(snap.id)
    residuals = residual_returns(returns, sched, seed=seed)
    targets = forward_targets(returns, residuals, cal)
    features = compute_features(bars, FEATURES)
    characterise_features(features, sched)
    runs = start_sig_entries(features, sched, snap.id, seed=seed)
    inputs = rank_ic(features, targets, sched)
    return test_features(inputs, runs)
```

## PL-21: Final verdict
**Entry:** `packages/lab/src/qrp_lab/pipelines/pl21_verdict.py::write_verdict` · **Trigger:** `qrp lab verdict` · **Part of:** PL-08
**Covers:** VERD-01–VERD-03

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `entries` | engine | stage, family → LedgerEntry | PL-17 as built: every `S4` entry, failed, incomplete and abandoned ones included | VERD-01 |
| 2 | `benchmark` | rankings.benchmark | → BacktestResult | read the stored PORT-05 result; `LedgerRefused` before PL-19 | VERD-01 |
| 3 | `judge` | verdict.judge | LedgerEntry, BacktestResult → Verdict | a row per entry; winner iff walk-forward passed and the lockbox is not significantly worse | VERD-01, VERD-02 |
| 4 | `write_report` | verdict.report | Verdict → Verdict | `results/verdict.md`, first line the winners or "none"; runs even if S4 stopped early | VERD-03 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def write_verdict() -> Verdict:
    s4 = entries(stage="S4", family="S4")
    bench = benchmark()
    verdict = judge(s4, bench)
    return write_report(verdict)
```

## Open questions
- `top_estimator(cov_ranking())` in PL-19 needs a reader for the recorded PL-18 `Ranking` (routed with the type; assumed: `lab.cov_ranking()` reads `results/<entry>/ranking` through `engine.result`)
- Lockbox scores (WFWD-05) for FPORT and AGENT entries are taken in `models` (PL-25, PL-26); `judge` only reads `LedgerEntry.lockbox` (assumed, since `lab` may not import `models`)
- Which features make the ≥ 10 in `lab.FEATURES` is a build task with the rationale per feature (FEAT-03), not a step

# models — Pipelines

**Package:** models · **Status:** Draft · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Contract:** [contract.md](contract.md)

## PL-22: Forecaster training
**Entry:** `packages/models/src/qrp_models/pipelines/pl22_forecast.py::train_forecaster` · **Trigger:** `qrp models forecast --config <file> --entry H-NNNN` · **Part of:** PL-06
**Covers:** FCST-01–FCST-07, SIG-03, WFWD-03, WFWD-04, QR-07
**References:** RF-02 (build_bundle), RF-03 (forward_targets)

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `start_run` | engine | entry id → RunHandle | `engine.start_run` freezes the registration | LEDG-02 |
| 2 | `check_features` | windows.features | ForecastConfig → ForecastConfig | every feature in `lab.surviving_features()`, else `LedgerRefused` | SIG-03 |
| 3 | `schedule` | engine | ForecastConfig → Schedule | PL-14 as built | WFWD-01 |
| 4 | `bars_panel` | data | Schedule, ForecastConfig → BarsPanel | `data.bars_panel`; the contract's replay double until the system task | PANEL-01 |
| 5 | `returns_panel` | data | Schedule, ForecastConfig → ReturnsPanel | PL-11 as built | PANEL-01 |
| 6 | `calendar` | data | ForecastConfig → Calendar | `data.calendar` | DATA-05 |
| 7 | `forward_targets` | lab | ReturnsPanel, Calendar → Targets | `lab.forward_targets`, horizons from the config | FCST-01 |
| 8 | `compute_features` | lab | BarsPanel, ForecastConfig → FeaturePanel | `lab.compute_features` on the config's features | SIG-03 |
| 9 | `build_bundle` | windows.samples | BarsPanel, FeaturePanel, Targets, Schedule, ForecastConfig → Bundle | per period: `training_window(horizon=max)`, `guard` on `label_end`, point-in-time sectors with `unknown`, vocabulary and normalisation from the window; as RF-02, purged and gap-checked | FCST-01–FCST-04, WFWD-03, WFWD-04 |
| 10 | `make_trainer` | forecasters.factory | ForecastConfig → Trainer | `device` cpu → `LocalTrainer`, cuda → `GpuTrainer` (two real implementations, AD-18) | FCST-07 |
| 11 | `LocalTrainer` | forecasters.local | ForecastConfig → Trainer | deterministic torch, seeded; a one-layer LSTM for both `model` values | FCST-07 |
| 12 | `GpuTrainer` | gpu.trainer | host, rate → Trainer | PL-23 as built | QR-02, QR-05 |
| 13 | `train_models` | forecasters.train | Bundle, Trainer → ForecastRun | fit per period, forecasts to `results/<entry>/forecasts.parquet`; `no_sample` counted | FCST-07 |
| 14 | `run_baselines` | forecasters.baselines | Bundle, ForecastRun → ForecastRun | trailing 63-day vol, EWMA vol, zero return | FCST-05, FCST-06 |
| 15 | `test_forecasts` | forecasters.tests | ForecastRun, RunHandle → ForecastRun | per-period loss vs each baseline into `engine.run_test` (`FCST-VOL` or `FCST-RET`), `record_outcome` | FCST-05, FCST-06 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def train_forecaster(config: ForecastConfig, *, entry: str) -> ForecastRun:
    run = start_run(entry)
    config = check_features(config)
    sched = schedule(config.snapshot)
    bars = bars_panel(sched, config)
    returns = returns_panel(sched, config)
    cal = calendar(config.snapshot)
    targets = forward_targets(returns, cal, horizons=config.horizons)
    features = compute_features(bars, config.features)
    bundle = build_bundle(bars, features, targets, sched, config)
    trainer = make_trainer(config)
    forecast = train_models(bundle, trainer)
    forecast = run_baselines(bundle, forecast)
    return test_forecasts(forecast, run)
```

## PL-23: GPU training run
**Entry:** `packages/models/src/qrp_models/pipelines/pl23_ship_bundle.py::ship_bundle` · **Trigger:** `GpuTrainer` in PL-22, or `qrp models gpu-train <bundle> --host H --rate R` · **Part of:** PL-06
**Covers:** QR-02, QR-05

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `open_rental` | gpu.budget | Bundle, host, rate → Rental | month's logged spend + estimate ≤ $100, else `BudgetExceeded` | QR-02 |
| 2 | `copy_bundle` | gpu.ssh | Bundle, Rental → Rental | `rsync` over SSH; derived arrays only | QR-05 |
| 3 | `train_remote` | gpu.remote | Bundle, Rental → ForecastRun | `ssh host qrp models train-bundle` (PL-24), copy back checkpoints and forecasts | FCST-07 |
| 4 | `teardown` | gpu.teardown | Rental → Rental | delete bundle and checkpoints, verify by listing; `TeardownUnverified` flags the rental; runs in a `finally` (AD-18) | QR-05 |
| 5 | `log_rental` | gpu.log | Rental → Rental | hours × rate to `store/gpu_log.sqlite` | QR-02 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def ship_bundle(bundle: Bundle, *, host: str, rate_per_hour: float) -> ForecastRun:
    rental = open_rental(bundle, host, rate_per_hour)
    try:
        rental = copy_bundle(bundle, rental)
        return train_remote(bundle, rental)
    finally:
        log_rental(teardown(rental))
```

## PL-24: Train a shipped bundle

| 3 | `train_models` | forecasters.train | Bundle, Trainer → ForecastRun | as PL-22 | FCST-07 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def run_train_bundle(path: Path) -> ForecastRun:
    bundle = load_bundle(path)
    trainer = LocalTrainer(bundle.config)
    return train_models(bundle, trainer)
```

## PL-25: Forecast-driven portfolio
**Entry:** `packages/models/src/qrp_models/pipelines/pl25_fport.py::run_fport` · **Trigger:** `qrp models fport --forecast H-NNNN --kind vol_minvar|return_risk_controlled --entry H-NNNN` · **Part of:** PL-07
**Covers:** FPORT-01–FPORT-03, WFWD-05

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `start_run` | engine | entry id → RunHandle | as PL-22 | LEDG-02 |
| 2 | `schedule` | engine | ForecastRun → Schedule | PL-14 as built | WFWD-01 |
| 3 | `benchmark` | lab | → BacktestResult | `lab.benchmark` | FPORT-01, FPORT-02 |
| 4 | `fport_weights` | fport.weights | ForecastRun, Schedule, kind → Weights | `vol_minvar`: `lab.min_variance` on forecast vols × training-window correlation; `return_risk_controlled`: top-forecast stocks, min-variance weighted | FPORT-01–FPORT-03 |
| 5 | `backtest` | engine | Weights, Schedule, ForecastRun → BacktestResult | PL-15 as built | FPORT-03 |
| 6 | `test_vs_benchmark` | fport.tests | BacktestResult, RunHandle → TestOutcome | paired variance (FPORT-01) or after-cost Sharpe (FPORT-02) in family `S4`; `built_on_failed_forecaster` label | FPORT-01, FPORT-02 |
| 7 | `score_lockbox` | fport.lockbox | RunHandle, ForecastRun → TestOutcome | `engine.score_lockbox` once, after the walk-forward outcome is recorded | WFWD-05 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def run_fport(forecast: ForecastRun, *, kind: str, entry: str) -> BacktestResult:
    run = start_run(entry)
    sched = schedule(forecast)
    bench = benchmark()
    weights = fport_weights(forecast, sched, kind)
    result = backtest(weights, sched, forecast)
    test_vs_benchmark(result, bench, run)
    score_lockbox(run, forecast)
    return result
```

## PL-26: Agent configuration
**Entry:** `packages/models/src/qrp_models/pipelines/pl26_agent.py::train_agent` · **Trigger:** `qrp models agent --config <file> --entry H-NNNN` (the line's entry) · **Part of:** PL-07
**Covers:** AGENT-01–AGENT-05, WFWD-05

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `start_config` | agents.entries | AgentConfig, entry id → RunHandle | register the configuration under the line (`parent`) and start it; `LedgerRefused` for a 21st or a stopped line | AGENT-02, AGENT-04 |
| 2 | `schedule` | engine | AgentConfig → Schedule | PL-14 as built | WFWD-01 |
| 3 | `returns_panel` | data | Schedule, AgentConfig → ReturnsPanel | PL-11 as built | PANEL-01 |
| 4 | `benchmark` | lab | → BacktestResult | `lab.benchmark` | AGENT-03 |
| 5 | `agent_weights` | agents.train | AgentConfig, ReturnsPanel, Schedule, RunHandle → Weights | per period: window, `guard`, a gymnasium market replaying window returns with 10 bp costs, PPO (stable-baselines3); softmax → `project_capped`; non-finite loss → `mark_incomplete(counts_as_failed=True)` | AGENT-01, AGENT-05 |
| 6 | `backtest` | engine | Weights, Schedule, AgentConfig → BacktestResult | PL-15 as built | AGENT-05 |
| 7 | `test_vs_benchmark` | agents.tests | BacktestResult, RunHandle → TestOutcome | after-cost Sharpe paired with the benchmark in family `S4`; line stopped when its budget is spent with no pass | AGENT-03, AGENT-04 |
| 8 | `score_lockbox` | agents.lockbox | RunHandle, AgentConfig → TestOutcome | `engine.score_lockbox` once: retrain on the window before the lockbox, backtest it | WFWD-05 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def train_agent(config: AgentConfig, *, entry: str) -> BacktestResult:
    run = start_config(config, entry)
    sched = schedule(config.snapshot)
    returns = returns_panel(sched, config)
    bench = benchmark()
    weights = agent_weights(config, returns, sched, run)
    result = backtest(weights, sched, config.snapshot)
    test_vs_benchmark(result, bench, run)
    score_lockbox(run, config)
    return result
```

## Open questions
- FPORT lockbox scoring needs forecasts on the lockbox dates, which `ForecastRun` doesn't cover (assumed: `score_lockbox` refits the forecaster on the window before the lockbox; may need a `ForecastRun` field, for architecture-design)
- PL-23's `try`/`finally` is a decision-backed exception (AD-18: teardown always runs)
- `qrp models gpu-budget` prints the month's logged spend from `gpu.budget` (assumed: no pipeline of its own)
- The TFT-lite model is an upgrade of `train_models`, not a new step; the spine trains the LSTM for both `model` values

zero return | FCST-05, FCST-06 |\n| 15 | `test_forecasts` | forecasters.tests | ForecastRun, RunHandle → ForecastRun | per-period loss vs each baseline into `engine.run_test` (`FCST-VOL` or `FCST-RET`), `record_outcome` | FCST-05, FCST-06 |\n\n### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```python\ndef train_forecaster(config: ForecastConfig, *, entry: str) -> ForecastRun:\n # server — Pipelines

**Package:** server · **Status:** Draft · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Contract:** [contract.md](contract.md)

Each route is one pipeline: the `Host` check, a `routes` read that maps `data` errors to `ErrorBody`, and a `wire` body. `qrp serve` (in `__main__.py`) mounts every route's entry on uvicorn bound to `127.0.0.1`.

## PL-27: Membership on a date
**Entry:** `packages/server/src/qrp_server/pipelines/pl27_membership.py::get_membership` · **Trigger:** `GET /api/membership?date=` · **Part of:** PL-02
**Covers:** EXPL-01, QR-04, QR-08

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `check_host` | guard.host | Host header → ErrorBody | raises 403 `ErrorBody(bad_request)` unless the host is loopback | QR-04 |
| 2 | `read_membership` | routes.membership | date, snapshot → Membership | `data.membership_as_of`; `CoverageError` → 422 `out_of_coverage`, `SnapshotError` → 409 `no_dataset` | EXPL-01, QR-08 |
| 3 | `to_membership_body` | wire.bodies | Membership → MembershipBody | field for field, dates as ISO strings | QR-06 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```python
def get_membership(request: Request, date: date, snapshot: str | None = None) -> MembershipBody:
    check_host(request)
    membership = read_membership(date, snapshot)
    return to_membership_body(membership)
```

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## PL-34: Live run stream
**Entry:** `packages/server/src/qrp_server/pipelines/pl34_stream.py::stream_run` · **Trigger:** `GET /api/runs/{id}/events` · **Part of:** PL-01

| Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `check_host` | guard.host | Host header → ErrorBody | raises 403 `ErrorBody(bad_request)` unless the host is loopback | QR-04 |\n| 2 | `read_membership` | routes.membership | date, snapshot → Membership | `data.membership_as_of`; `CoverageError` → 422 `out_of_coverage`, `SnapshotError` → 409 `no_dataset` | EXPL-01, QR-08 |\n| 3 | `to_membership_body` | wire.bodies | Membership → MembershipBody | field for field, dates as ISO strings | QR-06 |\n\n### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```python\ndef get_membership(request: Request, date: date, snapshot: str | None = None) -> MembershipBody:\n    check_host(request)\n    membership = read_membership(date, snapshot)\n    return to_membership_body(membership)\n```\n\n## PL-28: Stock search\n**Entry:** `packages/server/src/qrp_server/pipelines/pl28_search.py::search_stocks` · **Trigger:** `GET /api/stocks?q=` · **Part of:** PL-02\n**Covers:** EXPL-02, QR-04\n\n| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `check_host` | guard.host | Host header → ErrorBody | as PL-27 | QR-04 |\n| 2 | `read_matches` | routes.stocks | query, snapshot → StockRef | `data.find_stock`, every ticker ever used, delisted included | EXPL-02 |\n| 3 | `to_stock_ref_bodies` | wire.bodies | StockRef → StockRefBody | one body per match | QR-06 |\n\n### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```python\ndef search_stocks(request: Request, q: str) -> list[StockRefBody]:\n    check_host(request)\n    matches = read_matches(q, None)\n    return to_stock_ref_bodies(matches)\n```\n\n## PL-29: Stock page\n**Entry:** `packages/server/src/qrp_server/pipelines/pl29_stock.py::get_stock` · **Trigger:** `GET /api/stocks/{stock}` · **Part of:** PL-02\n**Covers:** EXPL-02, EXPL-03, QR-04, QR-08\n\n| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `check_host` | guard.host | Host header → ErrorBody | as PL-27 | QR-04 |\n| 2 | `read_stock` | routes.stocks | StockId, snapshot → StockHistory | `data.stock_history` (PL-13) behind an LRU of 50 pages; `KeyError` → 404 `not_found` | EXPL-02, QR-08 |\n| 3 | `to_stock_body` | wire.bodies | StockHistory → StockBody | columnar arrays, `null` for missing bars, actions and memberships | EXPL-03, QR-06 |\n\n### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```python\ndef get_stock(request: Request, stock: int, snapshot: str | None = None) -> StockBody:\n    check_host(request)\n    history = read_stock(StockId(stock), snapshot)\n    return to_stock_body(history)\n```\n\n## PL-30: Coverage\n**Entry:** `packages/server/src/qrp_server/pipelines/pl30_coverage.py::get_coverage` · **Trigger:** `GET /api/coverage` · **Part of:** PL-02\n**Covers:** EXPL-05, QR-04\n\n| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `check_host` | guard.host | Host header → ErrorBody | as PL-27 | QR-04 |\n| 2 | `read_coverage` | routes.coverage | snapshot → CoverageBody | `data.coverage` as columnar members and missing-bar counts per session | EXPL-05, QR-06 |\n\n### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```python\ndef get_coverage(request: Request, snapshot: str | None = None) -> CoverageBody:\n    check_host(request)\n    return read_coverage(snapshot)\n```\n\n## PL-31: Snapshot list\n**Entry:** `packages/server/src/qrp_server/pipelines/pl31_snapshots.py::list_snapshots` · **Trigger:** `GET /api/snapshots` · **Part of:** PL-02\n**Covers:** EXPL-04, QR-04\n\n| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `check_host` | guard.host | Host header → ErrorBody | as PL-27 | QR-04 |\n| 2 | `read_snapshots` | routes.snapshots | → Snapshot | `data.list_snapshots` and `data.current_snapshot` (none before the first promotion) | EXPL-04 |\n| 3 | `to_snapshots_body` | wire.bodies | Snapshot → SnapshotsBody | newest first, current marked | QR-06 |\n\n### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```python\ndef list_snapshots(request: Request) -> SnapshotsBody:\n    check_host(request)\n    snapshots = read_snapshots()\n    return to_snapshots_body(snapshots)\n```\n\n## PL-32: Validation issues\n**Entry:** `packages/server/src/qrp_server/pipelines/pl32_issues.py::get_issues` · **Trigger:** `GET /api/snapshots/{snapshot}/issues?check=&stock=` · **Part of:** PL-02\n**Covers:** EXPL-04, QR-04\n\n| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `check_host` | guard.host | Host header → ErrorBody | as PL-27 | QR-04 |\n| 2 | `read_issues` | routes.issues | SnapshotId, check, stock → Issue | `data.issues` filtered; never validated → `validated=false`, no rows | EXPL-04 |\n| 3 | `to_issues_body` | wire.bodies | Issue → IssuesBody | rows plus counts by check and the ticker on each date | QR-06 |\n\n### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```python\ndef get_issues(request: Request, snapshot: str, check: str | None = None, stock: int | None = None) -> IssuesBody:\n    check_host(request)\n    issues = read_issues(SnapshotId(snapshot), check, stock)\n    return to_issues_body(issues)\n```\n\n## PL-33: Run list\n**Entry:** `packages/server/src/qrp_server/pipelines/pl33_runs.py::list_runs` · **Trigger:** `GET /api/runs`, `GET /api/runs/{id}` · **Part of:** PL-01\n**Covers:** RUNS-03, QR-04\n\n| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `check_host` | guard.host | Host header → ErrorBody | as PL-27 | QR-04 |\n| 2 | `run_state` | live.state | run id → RunEventBody | a `state` event: the `RunBody` and every failure so far from `data.run_events(after_seq=0)` | RUNS-04 |\n| 3 | `follow` | live.follow | run id, RunEventBody → RunEventBody | poll `data.run_events` by `seq` every 0.5 s over SSE; `stopped` within 10 s of the last heartbeat; ends on `finished` | RUNS-01, RUNS-02 |\n\n### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```python\ndef stream_run(request: Request, run_id: str) -> EventSourceResponse:\n    check_host(request)\n    state = run_state(run_id)\n    return follow(run_id, state)\n```\n\n## PL-35: Export the wire schema\n**Entry:** `packages/server/src/qrp_server/pipelines/pl35_export_schema.py::run_export_schema` · **Trigger:** `qrp server export-schema` (CI runs it before the app's wire check)\n**Covers:** QR-06\n\n| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | `export_schema` | wire.schema | ErrorBody, MembershipBody, StockBody … RunEventBody → WireSchema | pydantic `model_json_schema` of every body, written to `packages/app/src/wire/schema.json` | QR-06 |\n\n### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```python\ndef run_export_schema(out: Path = APP_SCHEMA_PATH) -> WireSchema:\n    return export_schema(out)\n```\n\n## Planned layout (pre-build — removed by spine task)\n```\npackages/server/src/qrp_server/\n  __init__.py     contract exports only\n  __main__.py     CLI: serve (uvicorn on 127.0.0.1, mounts each route entry), export-schema\n  pipelines/      pl27_membership.py  pl28_search.py  pl29_stock.py  pl30_coverage.py  pl31_snapshots.py\n                  pl32_issues.py  pl33_runs.py  pl34_stream.py  pl35_export_schema.py\n  guard/          __init__.py  host.py\n  routes/         __init__.py  membership.py  stocks.py  coverage.py  snapshots.py  issues.py  runs.py  errors.py (data error → ErrorBody)\n  live/           __init__.py  state.py  follow.py\n  wire/           __init__.py  bodies.py  schema.py\ntests/intent/server/  tests/unit/server/\n```\n\n## Open questions\n- The `Host` check is a step call in every route rather than middleware, so each route's sketch shows it (assumed: a raised 403 stops the route)\n- `routes/errors.py` holds the shared `data` error → `ErrorBody` mapping each read step uses (assumed: a helper of `routes`, on no step of its own)\n\n## Retired\n"

# app — Pipelines

**Package:** app · **Status:** Draft · **Last updated:** 2026-10-17 · **Contract:** [contract.md](contract.md)

Each browser route in `app.routes` is one pipeline: a `client` hook returning a generated wire body, an optional `charts` series, and the page that renders it. Every page shows the "no dataset yet" state, linking to `/runs`, on `ErrorBody(no_dataset)`.

## PL-36: Membership page
**Entry:** `packages/app/src/pipelines/pl36-membership.tsx::MembershipPage` · **Trigger:** route `/membership?date=YYYY-MM-DD` · **Part of:** PL-02
**Covers:** EXPL-01, EXPL-06, QR-08

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `useMembership` | client.queries | date → MembershipBody | TanStack Query on `server.get_membership` | EXPL-01, QR-08 |
| 2 | `MembershipView` | explorer.membership | MembershipBody → page | date picker, "resolved to <used>" note, a sortable member table linking to stock pages | EXPL-01, EXPL-06 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```tsx
export function MembershipPage({ date }: { date: string }) {
  const body = useMembership(date);
  return <MembershipView body={body} />;
}
```

## PL-37: Stock search
**Entry:** `packages/app/src/pipelines/pl37-search.tsx::SearchPage` · **Trigger:** route `/stocks?q=` · **Part of:** PL-02
**Covers:** EXPL-02

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `useStockSearch` | client.queries | query → StockRefBody | TanStack Query on `server.search_stocks`, debounced | EXPL-02 |
| 2 | `SearchView` | explorer.search | StockRefBody → page | one row per company with its ticker spans and delisting date | EXPL-02, EXPL-06 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```tsx
export function SearchPage({ q }: { q: string }) {
  const matches = useStockSearch(q);
  return <SearchView matches={matches} />;
}
```

## PL-38: Stock page
**Entry:** `packages/app/src/pipelines/pl38-stock.tsx::StockPage` · **Trigger:** route `/stocks/:stock` · **Part of:** PL-02
**Covers:** EXPL-02, EXPL-03, EXPL-06, QR-08

| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `useStock` | client.queries | StockId → StockBody | TanStack Query on `server.get_stock`, cached per stock | EXPL-02, QR-08 |
| 2 | `toStockSeries` | charts.series | StockBody → Series | typed arrays straight from the columns; `null` kept so lines break at gaps | EXPL-03 |
| 3 | `StockView` | explorer.stock | StockBody, Series → page | Observable Plot price, return and volume panels with split, dividend, membership and delisting marks, light and dark, with the `dataviz` and `frontend-design` skills | EXPL-03, EXPL-06 |

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```tsx
export function StockPage({ stock }: { stock: number }) {
  const body = useStock(stock);
  const series = toStockSeries(body);
  return <StockView body={body} series={series} />;
}
```

## PL-43: Wire check

### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
```ts
export async function checkWire(): Promise<void> {
  const schema = await generateWire(SCHEMA_PATH);
  await checkFixtures(schema);
}
```

## Open questions
- Every page's last step renders a page, and `generateWire`/`checkFixtures` write files: `dt lint` (`step-io-types`) wants a contract type in every Out, but steps.md allows a plain final result (assumed: plain; see the gate)
- `main.tsx` (the router) sits on no step, like a Python `__main__.py` (assumed allowed)
- The chart components live in `charts/plots/` as helpers of `toStockSeries`/`toCoverageSeries`'s owner, drawn by the explorer views (assumed: the views import them; they have no step of their own)

full text
I've drafted `pipelines.md` for all six packages: 35 package pipelines, PL-09 to PL-43, all Status: Draft. When you said "approved" there was no `pipelines.md` yet, so I took it as approval to go ahead. I built the pipelines from the eight system workflows (PL-01 to PL-08) in the approved root architecture rather than asking you about workflows again. These docs still need your approval.

`dt status` now shows every package as `current`: every spec ID and every component lands on a step. `dt lint` still reports errors, and none of them can be fixed inside `pipelines.md`:
- **Missing contract types (61 errors):** steps name types the contracts don't define yet. I routed 10 of them to architecture-design; they clear when it revises the contracts.
- **Plain final outputs (9 errors):** the app's page-rendering steps and the wire-check script end in a rendered page or a file, not a contract type. The lint rule wants a contract type in every step's output; the step rules allow a plain result in the last step. See question 1.
- **Contract gaps (11 errors):** seven contract functions have no `Boundary` line and four types have no `Cases` line. Some predate this work, and the contracts aren't mine to edit, so I routed this too.

## What was written
**data**
- **PL-09 Download** · `qrp data download --full|--update [--resume]` · open_run → make_sources (Sharadar, FRED and EDGAR sources, plus a synthetic stand-in while PB-01 and PB-02 are unanswered) → fetch_universe → download_stocks → download_series → stage_snapshot → close_run
- **PL-10 Validate and promote** · `qrp data validate <snap>`, also run by `download` · six checks → decide → promote_snapshot → quality report (written with the `data-analysis` skill) → close_run
- **PL-11, PL-12, PL-13: returns, membership and stock-history reads** · each resolves the snapshot, gets the calendar, then does its read

**engine**
- **PL-14 Schedule** · calendar → tile_periods
- **PL-15 Backtest** · returns_panel → check_all → simulate
- **PL-16 Run a registered test** · match_registration → family_inputs → test_hypothesis (with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill) → write_result
- **PL-17 List the ledger** · `qrp engine ledger list`

**lab**
- **PL-18 Covariance ranking** · characterise factors, denoising and estimators → 4 estimator classes → walk-forward weights → backtest → tests → publish_ranking
- **PL-19 Classical portfolios** · read the ranking → top_estimator → four portfolios → backtest → tests → store the benchmark
- **PL-20 Feature significance** · residuals → targets → features → characterise → rank IC → tests
- **PL-21 Verdict** · entries → benchmark → judge → write_report

**models**
- **PL-22 Forecaster training** · features gate → targets and features → build_bundle → make_trainer (Local or GPU) → train → baselines → tests
- **PL-23 GPU run** · budget → copy → remote train → teardown → log
- **PL-24 Train a shipped bundle** · runs on the GPU host
- **PL-25 Forecast portfolio** and **PL-26 Agent configuration** · weights → backtest → test against the benchmark → lockbox score

**server**
- **PL-27 to PL-34** · one pipeline per route: host check → `routes` read → `wire` body. Live runs use run_state → follow over SSE.
- **PL-35 Export wire schema**

**app**
- **PL-36 to PL-42** · one pipeline per browser route: query hook → (series) → view
- **PL-43 Wire check** · `npm run check:wire`

**Layout:** each package gets a `pipelines/plNN_*.py` (or `.tsx`) entry module per pipeline, one module per step owner and a `__main__.py` for its commands. Tests go in `tests/intent/<pkg>/` and `tests/unit/<pkg>/`. I appended the calling PL IDs to the `Used by:` lines in five contracts (data, engine, lab, models, server).

## Assumptions
- **Workflows:** taken from the root PL-01 to PL-08. The data reads, engine calls and server routes each became their own package pipeline, because each is a distinct entry other code calls.
- **Entry names:** they keep the approved contract names (`validate_snapshot`, `returns_panel`, `train_agent`…) instead of `run_*`.
- **Validation and reports:** `qrp data download` runs PL-10 straight after staging. The quality report is written for rejected snapshots too.
- **Spine choices:** an LSTM stands in for both model types until the TFT upgrade. The test spine is a paired sign-flip permutation test with Holm correction. Risk parity counts as a failed period when the 5% cap binds.
- **Agents:** `train_agent`'s `entry` is the agent line's entry, and each configuration registers under it.
- **Exceptions:** the GPU teardown runs in a `finally` (allowed by AD-18). The server's host check is a step call in every route rather than middleware.

## Open questions
1. **Lint conflict:** should the final render or file steps keep plain outputs (my assumption), or should each get a contract type?
2. **FPORT lockbox scoring:** `ForecastRun` has no forecasts for the lockbox dates. I assumed the scorer refits the forecaster, and routed it.
3. **Where `qrp` lives:** no package may import all the others. I assumed each package's `__main__` handles its own subcommands, and routed it.
4. Smaller questions are at the bottom of each `pipelines.md`.

## Uncovered
None. Every spec ID and every component lands on a step.

Do you approve these, or want changes? On approval, architecture-design's Revise comes next to add the 10 routed types, the contract fixes and the two design questions.

`dt status` now shows every package as `current`: every spec ID and every component lands on a step. `dt lint` still reports errors, and none of them can be fixed inside `pipelines.md`:
- **Missing contract types (61 errors):** steps name types the contracts don't define yet. I routed 10 of them to architecture-design; they clear when it revises the contracts.
- **Plain final outputs (9 errors):** the app's page-rendering steps and the wire-check script end in a rendered page or a file, not a contract type. The lint rule wants a contract type in every step's output; the step rules allow a plain result in the last step. See question 1.
- **Contract gaps (11 errors):** seven contract functions have no `Boundary` line and four types have no `Cases` line. Some predate this work, and the contracts aren't mine to edit, so I routed this too.

## What was written
**data**
- **PL-09 Download** · `qrp data download --full|--update [--resume]` · open_run → make_sources (Sharadar, FRED and EDGAR sources, plus a synthetic stand-in while PB-01 and PB-02 are unanswered) → fetch_universe → download_stocks → download_series → stage_snapshot → close_run
- **PL-10 Validate and promote** · `qrp data validate <snap>`, also run by `download` · six checks → decide → promote_snapshot → quality report (written with the `data-analysis` skill) → close_run
- **PL-11, PL-12, PL-13: returns, membership and stock-history reads** · each resolves the snapshot, gets the calendar, then does its read

**engine**
- **PL-14 Schedule** · calendar → tile_periods
- **PL-15 Backtest** · returns_panel → check_all → simulate
- **PL-16 Run a registered test** · match_registration → family_inputs → test_hypothesis (with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill) → write_result
- **PL-17 List the ledger** · `qrp engine ledger list`

**lab**
- **PL-18 Covariance ranking** · characterise factors, denoising and estimators → 4 estimator classes → walk-forward weights → backtest → tests → publish_ranking
- **PL-19 Classical portfolios** · read the ranking → top_estimator → four portfolios → backtest → tests → store the benchmark
- **PL-20 Feature significance** · residuals → targets → features → characterise → rank IC → tests
- **PL-21 Verdict** · entries → benchmark → judge → write_report

**models**
- **PL-22 Forecaster training** · features gate → targets and features → build_bundle → make_trainer (Local or GPU) → train → baselines → tests
- **PL-23 GPU run** · budget → copy → remote train → teardown → log
- **PL-24 Train a shipped bundle** · runs on the GPU host
- **PL-25 Forecast portfolio** and **PL-26 Agent configuration** · weights → backtest → test against the benchmark → lockbox score

**server**
- **PL-27 to PL-34** · one pipeline per route: host check → `routes` read → `wire` body. Live runs use run_state → follow over SSE.
- **PL-35 Export wire schema**

**app**
- **PL-36 to PL-42** · one pipeline per browser route: query hook → (series) → view
- **PL-43 Wire check** · `npm run check:wire`

**Layout:** each package gets a `pipelines/plNN_*.py` (or `.tsx`) entry module per pipeline, one module per step owner and a `__main__.py` for its commands. Tests go in `tests/intent/<pkg>/` and `tests/unit/<pkg>/`. I appended the calling PL IDs to the `Used by:` lines in five contracts (data, engine, lab, models, server).

## Assumptions
- **Workflows:** taken from the root PL-01 to PL-08. The data reads, engine calls and server routes each became their own package pipeline, because each is a distinct entry other code calls.
- **Entry names:** they keep the approved contract names (`validate_snapshot`, `returns_panel`, `train_agent`…) instead of `run_*`.
- **Validation and reports:** `qrp data download` runs PL-10 straight after staging. The quality report is written for rejected snapshots too.
- **Spine choices:** an LSTM stands in for both model types until the TFT upgrade. The test spine is a paired sign-flip permutation test with Holm correction. Risk parity counts as a failed period when the 5% cap binds.
- **Agents:** `train_agent`'s `entry` is the agent line's entry, and each configuration registers under it.
- **Exceptions:** the GPU teardown runs in a `finally` (allowed by AD-18). The server's host check is a step call in every route rather than middleware.

## Open questions
1. **Lint conflict:** should the final render or file steps keep plain outputs (my assumption), or should each get a contract type?
2. **FPORT lockbox scoring:** `ForecastRun` has no forecasts for the lockbox dates. I assumed the scorer refits the forecaster, and routed it.
3. **Where `qrp` lives:** no package may import all the others. I assumed each package's `__main__` handles its own subcommands, and routed it.
4. Smaller questions are at the bottom of each `pipelines.md`.

## Uncovered
None. Every spec ID and every component lands on a step.
